= Engineering Model Go Repository Design
:toc:
:toclevels: 2
:sectnums:

== Introduction

This <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_ARCHITECTURE_MODEL,architecture models>> the engineering-model-go repository as a model-driven toolchain.
It captures authored functional intent, export surfaces, and verification-oriented traceability.
The <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> is intended to guide implementation work in this repository using stable IDs and support paths.

=== Scope and Assumptions

- <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_EM_FUNCTIONAL_GROUP,Functional Groups>> and <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_EM_FUNCTIONAL_UNIT,Functional Units>> are the stable authored design backbone.
- <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_EM_RUNTIME_ELEMENT,Runtime elements>> and <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_EM_CODE_ELEMENT,code elements>> are inferred from available IaC, deployment, and source artifacts.
- Inferred coverage is evidence-driven and may be partial when source metadata is missing.
- <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_EM_REQUIREMENT,Requirements>> and reference indexes are provided for traceability, not as primary narrative flow.

== How To Read This Document

This document is intentionally split into two layers:

1. Concept layer: functional and view-specific architecture narratives for human understanding.
2. Evidence layer: generated requirement traceability and inferred reference indexes.

Read in this order:

1. Architecture Intent View: stable authored architecture intent and ownership boundaries.
2. Communication / Deployment / Security Views: viewpoint-specific operational behavior.
3. Traceability View: requirement mapping, evidence coverage, and confidence.
4. Reference Appendix: supporting reference IDs and inferred-source links.

=== Diagram Color and Shape Legend

This legend applies to all Mermaid diagrams in this document.

[source,mermaid]
----
flowchart LR
  SYS["System"]:::system_boundary
  FG["Functional Group"]:::functional_group
  FU["Functional Unit"]:::functional_unit
  ACT(("Actor")):::actor
  REF["Referenced Element"]:::referenced_element
  IFACE[/"Interface"/]:::interface
  DATA[("Data Object")]:::data_object
  DEPT["Deployment Target"]:::deployment_target
  CTRL[["Control"]]:::control
  TB[/"Trust Boundary"\]:::trust_boundary
  ST(("State")):::state
  EVT(("Event")):::event
  FLOW{"Flow"}:::flow
  FSTEP>"Flow Step"]:::flow_step
  AV(("Attack Vector")):::attack_vector
  REQ["Requirement"]:::requirement
  VER["Verification Check"]:::verification
  RT["Runtime Element"]:::runtime_element
  DEP["Deployment Element"]:::deployment_element
  CODE["Code Element"]:::code_element

  SYS --> FG
  FG --> FU
  ACT --> FU
  FU --> REF
  FU --> IFACE
  FU --> DATA
  FU --> DEPT
  FU --> CTRL
  FU --> TB
  ST -->|triggered_by| EVT
  ST -->|guarded_by| CTRL
  FLOW --> FSTEP
  FSTEP -->|flow_ref| FU
  REQ -->|applies_to| FU
  VER -->|verifies| REQ
  VER -->|implemented_by| CODE
  FU -->|realizes| RT
  DEP --> RT
  AV --> FU
  RT -->|implemented_by| CODE
  REQ -->|code_trace| CODE

classDef system_boundary fill:#f5f5f5,stroke:#424242,stroke-width:2px,color:#212121;
classDef functional_group fill:#e8f5e9,stroke:#1b5e20,stroke-width:1px,color:#1b5e20;

classDef referenced_element fill:#f3e5f5,stroke:#6a1b9a,stroke-width:1px,color:#4a148c;

classDef data_object fill:#fff3e0,stroke:#ef6c00,stroke-width:2px,stroke-dasharray: 2 2,color:#e65100;
classDef deployment_target fill:#ede7f6,stroke:#5e35b1,stroke-width:1px,color:#4527a0;
classDef control fill:#fce4ec,stroke:#ad1457,stroke-width:1px,color:#880e4f;

[[SEC_VIEW_VIEW-ARCHITECTURE-INTENT]]
== Architecture Intent View

Show the stable authored architecture intent: capability areas, unit ownership boundaries, and intentional responsibility split.

=== What This View Answers


- What are the main capability areas?

- What are the stable units of responsibility and what does each unit own?

- How are responsibilities intentionally split?

=== System Boundary Diagram

What this diagram shows: explicit internal vs external scope. Functional units are inside the `System Boundary (Internal)` container, while actors and referenced dependencies are shown outside the boundary.

[source,mermaid]
----
flowchart LR
  ACT_ACT_ARCHITECTURE_AUTHOR(("Architecture Author")):::actor
  ACT_ACT_IMPLEMENTATION_ENGINEER(("Implementation Engineer")):::actor
  ACT_ACT_COMPLIANCE_ENGINEER(("Compliance Engineer")):::actor
  ACT_ACT_AI_AGENT(("AI Agent")):::actor
  ACT_ACT_CI_PIPELINE(("CI Pipeline")):::actor
  REF_REF_GO_TOOLCHAIN["Go Toolchain"]:::referenced_element
  REF_REF_THREAT_DRAGON_SCHEMAS["Threat Dragon Schemas"]:::referenced_element
  REF_REF_OPEN_OTM_SCHEMA["Open OTM Schema"]:::referenced_element
  REF_REF_STRUCTURIZR_VALIDATOR["Structurizr Validator"]:::referenced_element

=== Functional Groups and Units (View Scoped)



==== Model Authoring (FG-MODEL-AUTHORING)


Inputs and <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> loading responsibilities.



	Maintains authored architecture, <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirements>>, design, and <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_CATALOG,catalog>> handling.

none

[[SEC_VIEW_VIEW-SECURITY]]
== Security View

Show inferred exposure and dependency <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_RISK,risk>> points aligned to unit boundaries, focused on attack paths and security <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>>.

=== What This View Answers


- What are the main <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_TRUST_BOUNDARY,trust boundaries>> and attack paths?

- Which units are exposed and what security <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>> exists?

- Where is the threat <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> incomplete?

=== Coverage Gaps

Coverage summary: 3/12 units have direct <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>> coverage in this <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>>.


- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_ASCIIDOC_GENERATOR,AsciiDoc Generator>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_CLI_ORCHESTRATION,CLI Orchestration>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_LOBSTER_EXPORTER,LOBSTER Exporter>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODEL_CHANGE,Model Change>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_STRUCTURIZR_EXPORTER,Structurizr Exporter>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_SYSML_EXPORTER,SysML Exporter>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_TRLC_EXPORTER,TRLC Exporter>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_THREAT_EXPORTER,Threat Exporter>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.

- <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_VALIDATION_ENGINE,Validation Engine>> has no explicit authored attack-vector mapping yet; document technical and fraud/abuse threats even when mitigated.


=== Recommended Next Evidence Additions


- Add explicit authored attack-vector mappings for units currently marked without threat linkage.

- Add security signal ownership metadata (logs/alerts/<<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVENT,events>>) for unresolved observability <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>>.

- Map additional security-relevant dependencies and exposures into <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_AUTHORED_MAPPING,authored mappings>> for traceable coverage.

==== <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_AV_PATH_TRAVERSAL_IN_MCP,Path Traversal in MCP Calls>> (AV-PATH-TRAVERSAL-IN-MCP)

<<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_MODEL_LOADER,Model Loader>>, <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_VALIDATION_ENGINE,Validation Engine>>

code modules: cmd/<<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ENGMCP,engmcp>>, mcp

== Traceability Appendix

=== Requirement Details
[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_001]]
==== REQ-EMG-001

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_LOCAL_DEVELOPMENT,local development mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_MODEL_UPDATED,model updated event is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall validate <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_ARCHITECTURE_MODEL,architecture model>> before <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>.


NOTE: <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,Validation>> must gate all <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_GENERATION,generation>> flows.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_003]]
==== REQ-EMG-003

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_STATE_MODEL_VALID,model is valid>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall produce AsciiDoc architecture publication output without whitespace-only lines.


NOTE: Human-readable architecture output remains required and shall be clean for version <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_CONTROL,control>> and downstream processing.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_004]]
==== REQ-EMG-004

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_THREAT_EXPORT,threat export is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall export <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_DATA_THREAT_DRAGON_JSON,threat dragon json artifact>> plus open threat <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> artifact.


NOTE: Threat <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> interchange output contract.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_005]]
==== REQ-EMG-005

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_STRUCTURIZR_EXPORT,structurizr export is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall export <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_DATA_STRUCTURIZR_DSL,structurizr dsl artifact>>.


NOTE: <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_STRUCTURIZR_DSL,Structurizr DSL>> is exported for C4 visualization; the exported DSL is validated best-effort by the validate-all gauntlet when a <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_REF_STRUCTURIZR_VALIDATOR,Structurizr validator>> is available.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_009]]
==== REQ-EMG-009

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_CI_VALIDATION,ci validation mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall execute <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,validation>> plus tests before <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_ARTIFACT_GENERATED,artifact generated event is raised>>.


NOTE: CI installs only the runtimes required by <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,validation>>, uses mutually compatible exact <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_VALIDATOR,validator>> versions and supported command-line semantics for those versions, and configures dependency caches only when their native project metadata exists, so setup and <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,validation>> do not fail before the <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,validation>> gauntlet completes.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_010]]
==== REQ-EMG-010

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COND_REQUIREMENT_TRACE_COMPLETE,requirement trace is complete>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_MODEL_UPDATED,model updated event is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall infer traceability coverage from <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_TRACE_MARKER,trace markers>>.


NOTE: Links runtime, code, and test markers to modeled <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> coverage. Code and metadata scanning excludes dependency caches (node_modules, vendor) and dot-directories (.git, .engmod, tool caches) so coverage reflects only committed first-party source and stays reproducible.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_012]]
==== REQ-EMG-012

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall generate artifacts deterministically so that regeneration is reproducible.


NOTE: <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_GENERATION,Generation>> uses stable ordering and controlled metadata so maintained artifacts are bit-for-bit reproducible. The <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,validation>> gauntlet regenerates every maintained example and applies authoritative parsers or schemas where available plus semantic and idiomatic checks for each published format.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_013]]
==== REQ-EMG-013

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_TRACEABILITY_EXPORT,traceability export is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall export an OSCAL aggregate profile plus SSP plus assessment plan plus assessment results plus POA&M artifacts when the corresponding <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>> and remediation content is applicable.


NOTE: OSCAL documents use version 1.1.2 structures, deterministic metadata, resolvable sibling import links, aggregate every implemented <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>> framework, and omit inapplicable documents rather than emitting schema-invalid empty artifacts.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_014]]
==== REQ-EMG-014

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_ARCHITECTURE_DECISION_RECORDS,architecture decision records are authored>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall publish <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_DATA_ARCHITECTURE_DECISION_RECORDS,architecture decision records document>> linked from AsciiDoc architecture publication output.


NOTE: ADRs are first-class <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> content but are published separately from the main architecture narrative.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_015]]
==== REQ-EMG-015

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_GEMARA_EXPORT,gemara export is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall render OpenSSF Gemara <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> artifacts.


NOTE: Gemara is a first-class GRC rendering of the <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>>, validated against the published Gemara schemas via the go-gemara SDK.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_016]]
==== REQ-EMG-016

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall resolve the subsystem references declared by the parent system from local subdirectory paths.


NOTE: Recursive system-of-systems <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPOSITION,composition>>; local subdirectory references, with external git repositories cloned into the .engmod cache (<<REF_IDX-REQ_REQ_EMG_027,REQ-EMG-027>>, ADR-EMG-008, ADR-EMG-010).

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_017]]
==== REQ-EMG-017

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall reject any subsystem reference that resolves outside the <<REF_IDX-TB_TB_REPO_WORKSPACE,repository workspace boundary>>.


NOTE: Downward-only references remain within the workspace; mirrors the MCP path-boundary guardrail.

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall resolve cross-system references using subsystem-qualified identifiers.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_020]]
==== REQ-EMG-020

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall materialize parent-to-subsystem <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FU_ALLOCATION_TRACE,allocation trace>>ability in the composed output without modifying any subsystem <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>>.


NOTE: A subsystem is parent-agnostic; parent <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ALLOCATION,allocation>> is materialized only in composed artifacts, never written back to the child (ADR-EMG-009, ADR-EMG-011).

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_021]]
==== REQ-EMG-021

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall report each subsystem required <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_INTERFACE,interface>> that the parent system does not satisfy.


NOTE: Subsystems publish a provides-and-requires contract; the parent must satisfy each required <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_INTERFACE,interface>>.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_022]]
==== REQ-EMG-022

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall reject an <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ALLOCATION,allocation>> that targets a subsystem identifier not published in the subsystem contract.


NOTE: Parents may bind only to a subsystem's public contract surface, not its internal identifiers.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_023]]
==== REQ-EMG-023

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall flag any <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> that links to another <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> in the same document.


NOTE: <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,Requirements>> carry no tiers. A <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> is either delegated to a subsystem through a downward <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPOSITION,composition>> <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ALLOCATION,allocation>> or implemented here, and the delegation link alone tells the two apart. Linking <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirements>> to one another inside a document recreates tier coupling and is confusing across system boundaries, so it is flagged.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_024]]
==== REQ-EMG-024

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_HARDWARE_INTERFACES,hardware interfaces are authored>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall render the hardware to software <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_INTERFACE,interface>> <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ALLOCATION,allocation>> <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>>.


NOTE: Hardware items and hardware interfaces capture the HW/SW <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_INTERFACE,interface>> (ICD/IRS) for the interface-control <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>>.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_026]]
==== REQ-EMG-026

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall render in the <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>> the delegation link from each delegated <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> to the specific subsystem <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> that satisfies it.


NOTE: A provided contract entry names (ref) the subsystem <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> that realizes it, so a parent delegation traces to exactly the <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> that satisfies it. An allocated capability with no such ref is flagged as untraceable, and a ref to a <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> the subsystem does not define is rejected.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_027]]
==== REQ-EMG-027

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall resolve an external git subsystem reference by cloning the repository into the workspace .engmod cache.


NOTE: A subsystem may reference an external repository with git (and an optional rev and path). The <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> is cloned once into .engmod/subsystems/<id> — a gitignored, temporary cache reused while it points at the same repository — and resolved like a local subsystem, giving a full cross-repository composed <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>>. References stay downward-only.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_028]]
==== REQ-EMG-028

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall reject a code trace link that resolves to no <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> or <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> element.


NOTE: Code markers (TRLC-LINKS to a <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>>, ENGMODEL-LINKS to a <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> element) are existence-checked against the <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>>, not only shape-checked, so a link to a deleted or mistyped id is a dangling link and fails the build. A trace link is only checked for the <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> that owns its code (see <<REF_IDX-REQ_REQ_EMG_030,REQ-EMG-030>> scoping).

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_029]]
==== REQ-EMG-029

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall flag each <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> that is traced by neither code nor verification nor a downward delegation.


NOTE: An orphan <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> has no implementing code symbol, no verification, and is not delegated to a subsystem; it is a coverage gap surfaced as a warning so it is visible without breaking the build.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_030]]
==== REQ-EMG-030

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall export a machine-readable traceability matrix that links each <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> to its implementing code and verification and delegated subsystem <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>>.


NOTE: One consolidated matrix (JSON) replaces having to read it out of prose and diagrams. Code is attributed to the <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> whose architecture.yml is its nearest enclosing root, so a parent <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> never validates or counts a child <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>>'s trace links (monorepo scoping).

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_031]]
==== REQ-EMG-031

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_REQUIREMENTS_DELTA_RECEIVED,requirements delta is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall strictly validate <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_REQUIREMENTS_DELTA,requirements delta>> schema plus stable identifier operation uniqueness.


NOTE: Version 1 supports add, whole-entity update, and remove operations. Unknown fields, empty operations, duplicate operations, adds of existing IDs, and updates or removals of missing IDs are rejected.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_032]]
==== REQ-EMG-032

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_DRY_RUN,dry run mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_REQUIREMENTS_DELTA_RECEIVED,requirements delta is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall validate <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_CANDIDATE_ARCHITECTURE_MODEL,candidate architecture model>> without modifying <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_REQUIREMENTS_DOCUMENT,requirements document>>.


NOTE: The candidate reuses complete-model structural, <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_CATALOG,catalog>>, EARS, and internal-link <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,validation>> before any write is permitted.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_033]]
==== REQ-EMG-033

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_STATE_MODEL_VALID,model is valid>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_REQUIREMENTS_DELTA_APPLY_REQUESTED,requirements delta apply is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall atomically replace <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_REQUIREMENTS_DOCUMENT,requirements document>>.


NOTE: The YAML AST merger preserves unchanged nodes and comments. Atomic replacement preserves file permissions and refuses a candidate with blocking diagnostics.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_035]]
==== REQ-EMG-035

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_MODEL_UPDATED,model updated event is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall normalize <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_ARCHITECTURE_MODEL,architecture model>> plus <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_REQUIREMENTS_DOCUMENT,requirements document>> into <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_CANONICAL_SEMANTIC_MODEL,canonical semantic model>>.


NOTE: The <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_CANONICAL_SEMANTIC_MODEL,canonical semantic model>> is <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> v2 and <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_KERML,KerML>> aligned. Existing Engineering <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,Model>> concepts map into that <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> once; legacy YAML remains an input adapter during migration and must not create a parallel set of parts, interfaces, flows, <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirements>>, allocations, or views.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_036]]
==== REQ-EMG-036

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSML_EXPORT,sysml export is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall generate <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_DATA_SYSML_V2_MODEL,sysml v2 model artifact>> from <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_CANONICAL_SEMANTIC_MODEL,canonical semantic model>>.


NOTE: The exporter preserves stable identifiers and maps standard concepts to native <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> v2 or <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_KERML,KerML>> semantics. Engineering-specific concepts may use explicit typed <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> metadata definitions, but canonical data without a native target representation must be omitted rather than encoded in descriptions, opaque payloads, generic property bags, or sidecar files.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_037]]
==== REQ-EMG-037

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_CI_VALIDATION,ci validation mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall validate <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_DATA_SYSML_V2_MODEL,sysml v2 model artifact>> before <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_ARTIFACT_GENERATED,artifact generated event is raised>>.


NOTE: <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,Validation>> must report unsupported or lossy mappings and progress toward abstract-syntax, textual-syntax, semantic, and model-interchange conformance. A syntactically <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_VALID,valid>> subset export must not be described as a complete <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> v2 semantic superset.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_038]]
==== REQ-EMG-038

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSML_EXPORT,sysml export is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall validate <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_CANONICAL_SEMANTIC_MODEL,canonical semantic model>> against <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_SYSML_V2_METAMODEL,sysml v2 metamodel>>.


NOTE: The pinned formal <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> 2.0 and <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_KERML,KerML>> 1.0 metamodel is the source of truth for supported classes, properties, relationships, ownership, and multiplicities. Generated CUE constraints and semantic registries must not be maintained as independent handwritten copies.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_039]]
==== REQ-EMG-039

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_CI_VALIDATION,ci validation mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall reject incomplete <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_SYSML_V2_METAMODEL,sysml v2 metamodel>> coverage.


NOTE: Coverage is complete only when every normative metaclass and property in the pinned formal release is represented, explicitly excluded as derived, or implemented as a typed Engineering <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,Model>> extension with executable <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>>.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_040]]
==== REQ-EMG-040

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall preserve the deterministic native <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,sysml>> v2 source through project interchange round trip.


NOTE: Sysand owns normative KPAR packaging and reopening. Verification compares the reopened <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> source byte-for-byte with a fresh <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_PROJECTION,projection>> and validates both sources with the pinned official parser; it does not claim lossless reconstruction of canonical concepts omitted from <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>>.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_041]]
==== REQ-EMG-041

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_NAF_PROFILE,naf profile authoring is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_MODEL_UPDATED,model updated event is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall validate <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_NAF_V4_1,naf v4.1>> stakeholder plus concern plus viewpoint metadata against <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_CANONICAL_MODEL,canonical model>> references.


NOTE: NAF-specific YAML contains profile metadata only. Capability, service, logical, resource, behavior, <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_INTERFACE,interface>>, and <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> concepts remain represented once in the canonical <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> and <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_KERML,KerML>> aligned semantic graph.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_042]]
==== REQ-EMG-042

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_NAF_EXPORT,naf export is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall generate deterministic <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_NAF_V4_ARCHITECTURE,naf v4.1 architecture document>> from referenced <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_CANONICAL_MODEL,canonical model>> views.


NOTE: Each <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_NAF_ARCHITECTURE,NAF architecture>> product uses an official <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_NAF_V4_1,NAF v4.1>> viewpoint code and references an existing Engmod <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>> so generated content preserves canonical identities and relationships.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_043]]
==== REQ-EMG-043

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_CI_VALIDATION,ci validation mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall reject unsupported naf version plus unknown viewpoint code plus dangling profile reference before <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_ARTIFACT_GENERATED,artifact generated event is raised>>.


NOTE: <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VALIDATION,Validation>> covers stakeholder <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_ACTOR,actor>> bindings, concern ownership, architecture-product concern references, and Engmod <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>> references.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_044]]
==== REQ-EMG-044

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_MODEL_UPDATED,model updated event is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall load schema version 2 engmod manifest plus <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_CATALOG,catalog>> plus <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirements>> plus architecture plus behavior plus <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ACT_COMPLIANCE_ENGINEER,assurance>> plus <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>> plus views plus <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_DECISIONS_DOCUMENT,decisions documents>> into one <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_CANONICAL_SEMANTIC_MODEL,canonical semantic model>>.


NOTE: engmod.yml is the only <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> entry point and every document path is explicit. YAML is the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_AUTHORING,authoring>> format and CUE is the schema authority. <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,Requirements>> may carry format-neutral qualification metadata such as title, category, rationale, source references, verification methods and criteria, priority, criticality, status, derived disposition, and tags. Views own design narratives plus generic document definitions, document-control metadata, content selections, authored sections, and external references; architecture does not contain behavior, <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ACT_COMPLIANCE_ENGINEER,assurance>>, <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>>, dependency source, NAF, or projection-specific declarations.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_045]]
==== REQ-EMG-045

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_MCP_SERVER,mcp server is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_MCP_TOOL_CALL_RECEIVED,mcp tool call is received>> and <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COND_CATALOG_REFERENCES_RESOLVE,catalog references resolve successfully>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall return schema version 2 <<REF_IDX-DO_DO_MODEL_AUTHORING_CONTRACT,model authoring contract>>.


NOTE: The contract contains the manifest document map, authoritative CUE schema for each domain document, stable-ID and typed-relationship conventions, dependency publication aliases, <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_VALID,valid>> references, and recommended task-scoped <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_AUTHORING,authoring>> order.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_046]]
==== REQ-EMG-046

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_CI_VALIDATION,ci validation mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall reject <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> input that violates the schema version 2 manifest or any referenced domain document contract before <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_ARTIFACT_GENERATED,artifact generated event is raised>>.


NOTE: Missing documents, unsupported versions, unknown fields, duplicate concepts, <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_INVALID,invalid>> document-control values, incomplete derived <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> rationale, unresolved document content selections, unresolved local or qualified references, dependency publication violations, and cross-domain ownership violations are blocking.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_047]]
==== REQ-EMG-047

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall resolve each exact CUE module version from the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_OCI_REGISTRY,OCI registry>> configured by CUE_REGISTRY.


NOTE: Dependencies are declared only in engmod.yml with an alias, major-qualified module path, exact semantic version, and selected publications. The resolved module contains engmod.yml, its domain documents, and cue.mod/module.cue.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_048]]
==== REQ-EMG-048

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_MODEL_WORKSPACE_REPLACEMENT,a model workspace replacement is declared>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall resolve the matching manifest dependency from the replacement repository without changing module identity or selected publications.


NOTE: engmod.work.yml maps module paths to local repositories for offline multi-repository development. A generated engmod.lock.yml records exact module versions and OCI digests but is not an authored semantic document.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_049]]
==== REQ-EMG-049

While <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_MODE_CI_VALIDATION,ci validation mode is active>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall reject <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_INVALID,invalid>> manifest dependency plus publication plus <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_WORKSPACE_REPLACEMENT,workspace replacement>> plus qualified reference declarations.


NOTE: Every dependency requires one alias, one major-qualified module path, one exact version, and at least one exported publication. Aliases are unique, module identities match cue.mod/module.cue, workspace paths remain contained, imported references use alias double-colon stable-id syntax, and transitive entities are unavailable unless re-exported.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_050]]
==== REQ-EMG-050

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_EXAMPLE_VALIDATION_REQUESTED,example validation is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall validate a schema version 2 company portfolio composed from multiple product systems that reuse published hardware plus software plus security plus <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>> facets across repository boundaries.


NOTE: The reference workspace demonstrates explicit domain documents, dependency publication selection, qualified imported references, <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>> delegation, contract satisfaction, inherited company controls, technical security controls, employee lifecycle <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>>, provenance, exact OCI coordinates, local <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_WORKSPACE_REPLACEMENT,workspace replacements>>, and complete native format projections.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_051]]
==== REQ-EMG-051

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SYSTEM_COMPOSITION,system composition is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall import only explicitly selected dependency publications with qualified identity plus source provenance.


NOTE: Each dependency publication exposes typed architecture, behavior, <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ACT_COMPLIANCE_ENGINEER,assurance>>, <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>>, <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirement>>, and <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>> references. Imported entities retain module path, exact version, digest, source <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>>, and publication identity; unexported dependency entities remain inaccessible.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_052]]
==== REQ-EMG-052

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_SIX_VIEW_OUTPUT,six-view example output is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_GENERATION_RUN_REQUESTED,generation run is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall generate deterministic use-case plus logical plus process plus physical plus implementation plus deployment <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_VIEW,view>> artifacts and native <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> from one schema version 2 YAML bundle.


NOTE: The acceptance example preserves viewpoint separation, hierarchical capabilities, ordered typed interactions, nested hardware and software structures, ports, connections, engineering values, and software-to-hardware allocations. Mermaid sources and SVG renderings are generated without depending on authored <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYSML,SysML>> source files.

Where <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_FEAT_DO178C_DAL_C_READINESS,do-178c dal c readiness output is enabled>>, when <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_MODEL_UPDATED,model updated event is received>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall load a strict aviation <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ACT_COMPLIANCE_ENGINEER,assurance>> profile plus lifecycle data plus verification <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>> plus configuration baselines plus tool assessments plus structural coverage summaries.


NOTE: The <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_MODEL,model>> references DO-178C or ED-12C and customer-controlled licensed objective identifiers without reproducing normative objective text. It distinguishes declared, planned, executed, reviewed, accepted, not-applicable, and authority-accepted <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>> <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_STATE,states>>.

[[REF_REQ_REQ_EMG_056]]
==== REQ-EMG-056

When <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_EVT_EXAMPLE_VALIDATION_REQUESTED,example validation is requested>>, the <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_SYS_ENGINEERING_MODEL_GO,engineering model go>> shall validate a schema version 2 DAL C flight <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_CONTROL,control>> software example with placeholder planning plus <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_REQUIREMENT,requirements>> plus design plus code plus verification plus coverage plus configuration plus quality <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_ACT_COMPLIANCE_ENGINEER,assurance>> plus certification liaison <<REF_IDX-ENGMODEL_TERM_EVIDENCE,evidence>>.


NOTE: The example demonstrates realistic output shapes and intentionally open findings without claiming DO-178C <<REF_IDX-CATALOG_COMPLIANCE,compliance>> or certification.

== Gemara GRC Model

This chapter renders the model as OpenSSF Gemara (https://gemara.openssf.org) documents. Each artifact is built with the official `go-gemara` SDK types and validated against the published Gemara CUE schemas. Generate the full YAML set with `enggemara`; query it via the `gemara.*` MCP tools.

[cols="1,3,1",options="header"]
|===
|Gemara Layer |Artifact |Entries
|L1
|Vector Catalog
|4
|L1
|Principle Catalog
|5
|L1
|Guidance Catalog
|2
|L2
|Capability Catalog
|20
|L2
|Control Catalog
|5
|L2
|Threat Catalog
|2
|L3
|Risk Catalog
|3
|L3
|Policy
|2
|-
|Control→Threat Mapping
|3
|-
|Lexicon
|83
|L7
|Audit Log
|5
|L6
|Enforcement Log
|2
|===

=== Controls (L2)

[cols="2,4,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|Control |Objective |Group |Assessment Requirements
|CTRL-MCP-PATH-BOUNDARY
|Enforce repo-root path constraints and reject traversal paths in MCP tools.
|input-validation
|1
|CTRL-STRICT-MCP-INPUT-SCHEMA
|Enforce per-tool input schema and reject unknown arguments.
|input-validation
|1
|CTRL-TRACEABILITY-COVERAGE
|Require requirement-linked verification evidence for modeled behavior.
|assurance
|1
|CTRL-TRACE-LINK-INTEGRITY
|Reject code trace links (TRLC-LINKS, ENGMODEL-LINKS) that resolve to no requirement or model element, and export a consolidated traceability…
|assurance
|1
|CTRL-ARTIFACT-FRESHNESS-GATE
|Generate artifacts deterministically and fail continuous integration when regenerated documents differ from the committed artifacts.
|assurance
|1
|===

=== Threats (L2)

[cols="2,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Threat |Title |Group
|TS-MCP-PATH-TRAVERSAL
|MCP path traversal accesses non-repo files
|stride-tampering
|TS-TRACEABILITY-DRIFT
|Requirement traceability drifts from implementation
|stride-repudiation
|===

=== Risks (L3)

[cols="2,4,1",options="header"]
|===
|Risk |Title |Severity
|RISK-MCP-PATH-ESCAPE
|MCP path traversal exposes non-repository files
|High
|RISK-TRACEABILITY-DRIFT
|Traceability drift weakens assurance evidence
|Medium
|RISK-STALE-ARTIFACTS
|Stale generated artifacts misrepresent the system
|Low
|===
